00170: # Subtle procedural grain supplies worn boards without image textures.
00171: for obj in bpy.data.objects:
00172:     if obj.type!='MESH':
00173:         continue
00174:     ancestry=[]
00175:     p=obj
00176:     while p:
00177:         ancestry.append(p.name.lower())
00178:         p=p.parent
00179:     labels=' '.join(ancestry)
00180:     if 'plank_' in labels or 'oak locker' in labels or 'left stud' in labels or 'ceiling beam' in labels:
00181:         if not obj.data.materials:
00182:             continue
00183:         mat=obj.data.materials[0].copy()
00184:         obj.data.materials[0]=mat
00185:         mat.use_nodes=True
00186:         nt=mat.node_tree
00187:         bs=nt.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')
00188:         base=bs.inputs['Base Color'].default_value[:]
00189:         tex=nt.nodes.new('ShaderNodeTexNoise')
00190:         tex.inputs['Scale'].default_value=4
00191:         tex.inputs['Detail'].default_value=3
00192:         coord=nt.nodes.new('ShaderNodeTexCoord')
00193:         mapping=nt.nodes.new('ShaderNodeVectorMath')
00194:         mapping.operation='MULTIPLY'
00195:         mapping.inputs[1].default_value=(35,1.4,7)
00196:         nt.links.new(coord.outputs['Generated'],mapping.inputs[0])
00197:         nt.links.new(mapping.outputs[0],tex.inputs['Vector'])
00198:         ramp=nt.nodes.new('ShaderNodeValToRGB')
00199:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position=.20
00200:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color=tuple(v*.56 for v in base[:3])+(1,)
00201:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position=.78
00202:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color=tuple(min(v*1.4,1) for v in base[:3])+(1,)
00203:         nt.links.new(tex.outputs['Fac'],ramp.inputs[0])
00204:         nt.links.new(ramp.outputs[0],bs.inputs['Base Color'])
00205:         bump=nt.nodes.new('ShaderNodeBump')
00206:         bump.inputs['Strength'].default_value=.19
00207:         bump.inputs['Distance'].default_value=.018
00208:         nt.links.new(tex.outputs['Fac'],bump.inputs['Height'])

00210:         bs.inputs['Roughness'].default_value=.72

00184: # Materials are edited through semantic ancestors, with copies for wood.
00185: for obj in bpy.data.objects:
00186:     if obj.type!='MESH':
00187:         continue
00188:     ancestry=[]
00189:     p=obj
00190:     while p:
00191:         ancestry.append(p.name.lower())
00192:         p=p.parent
00193:     labels=' '.join(ancestry)
00194:     if any(s in labels for s in ['plank_','oak locker','left stud','ceiling beam']):
00195:         if not obj.data.materials:
00196:             continue
00197:         mat=obj.data.materials[0].copy()
00198:         obj.data.materials[0]=mat
00199:         mat.use_nodes=True
00200:         nt=mat.node_tree
00201:         bs=nt.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')
00202:         base=bs.inputs['Base Color'].default_value[:]

00007: # Observations: step 2 places the locker and cropped right bunk near the
00008: # reference landmarks. Its floor, ironwork and timbers remain much too pale.
00009: # Architectural edges and foreground perspective are imperfect but coherent.
00010: # Hypothesis: excessive fill light and light wood finishes flatten the scene.
00011: # Main correction family: materials and illumination. Expected effect:
00012: # dark worn floorboards, black iron silhouettes, yellow plaster and stronger
00013: # contact shadows. Camera, dimensions, placements and semantic groups remain.
00014: # Inventory: two left bunks, cropped right bunk, locker, fireplace, radiator,
00015: # bin, circular wall fitting, timber framing, plaster, ceiling and board floor.
00016: # Estimated landmarks: locker (307,174), hearth (401,192), aisle post (145,200).
00017: # Camera scale and distance remain uncertain monocular assumptions.
00018:

00020: linen = (.88,.89,.84)
00021: wood = (.14,.067,.026)
00022: 
00023: def B(parent,key,size,center,color):
00024:     return parent.include(key,box(size=size,center=center,color=color))
00025: 
00026: def R(parent,key,start,end,radius=.022,color=iron):
00027:     return parent.include(key,rod(radius=radius,start=start,end=end,color=color))
